003d716e	a	uniform sampler2D videoTexture;

003d71d1	a	void main()
003d71df	a	    vec2 srcTexelSize = 1.0 / srcResolution;
003d720c	a	    float x = srcTexelSize.x;
003d722a	a	    float y = srcTexelSize.y;
003d7249	a	    // Take 13 samples around current texel:
003d7276	a	    // a - b - c
003d7287	a	    // - j - k -
003d7298	a	    // d - e - f
003d72a9	a	    // - l - m -
003d72ba	a	    // g - h - i
003d72cb	a	    // === ('e' is the current texel) ===
003d72f5	a	    vec3 a = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x - 2*x, out_uvs.y + 2*y)).rgb;
003d7345	a	    vec3 b = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x,       out_uvs.y + 2*y)).rgb;
003d7395	a	    vec3 c = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x + 2*x, out_uvs.y + 2*y)).rgb;
003d73e6	a	    vec3 d = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x - 2*x, out_uvs.y)).rgb;
003d7430	a	    vec3 e = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x,       out_uvs.y)).rgb;
003d747a	a	    vec3 f = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x + 2*x, out_uvs.y)).rgb;
003d74c5	a	    vec3 g = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x - 2*x, out_uvs.y - 2*y)).rgb;
003d7515	a	    vec3 h = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x,       out_uvs.y - 2*y)).rgb;
003d7565	a	    vec3 i = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x + 2*x, out_uvs.y - 2*y)).rgb;
003d75b6	a	    vec3 j = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x - x, out_uvs.y + y)).rgb;
003d7602	a	    vec3 k = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x + x, out_uvs.y + y)).rgb;
003d764e	a	    vec3 l = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x - x, out_uvs.y - y)).rgb;
003d769a	a	    vec3 m = texture(videoTexture, vec2(out_uvs.x + x, out_uvs.y - y)).rgb;

003d770b	a	    // 0.5 + 0.125 + 0.125 + 0.125 + 0.125 = 1
003d773a	a	    // a,b,d,e * 0.125
003d7751	a	    // b,c,e,f * 0.125
003d7768	a	    // d,e,g,h * 0.125
003d777f	a	    // e,f,h,i * 0.125
003d7796	a	    // j,k,l,m * 0.5

003d77fe	a	    // so to have an energy preserving downsample we need to make some adjustments.
003d7852	a	    // The weights are the distributed, so that the sum of j,k,l,m (e.g.)
003d789c	a	    // contribute 0.5 to the final color output. The code below is written
003d78e7	a	    // to effectively yield this sum. We get:
003d7915	a	    // 0.125*5 + 0.03125*4 + 0.0625*4 = 1
003d793f	a	    vec3 downsample = e*0.125;
003d795e	a	    downsample += (a+c+g+i)*0.03125;
003d7983	a	    downsample += (b+d+f+h)*0.0625;
003d79a7	a	    downsample += (j+k+l+m)*0.125;
003d79cf	a	    colourOut = downsample;

003d8ce7	a	vec3 DoTonemap(vec3 result)

003d8df2	a	            break;
003d8e05	a	        case TONEMAP_MODE_HABLE:

003d8e5b	a	            break;
003d8e6e	a	        case TONEMAP_MODE_REINHARD:
003d8e92	a	            tonemap = result.rgb / (result.rgb + vec3(1.0));
003d8ecf	a	            break;
003d8ee2	a	        case TONEMAP_MODE_REINHARD_2:
003d8f08	a	            tonemap = reinhard2(result.rgb);
003d8f35	a	            break;
003d8f48	a	        case TONEMAP_MODE_CLAMP_CHROMA:

003d8ff5	a	            tonemap = mix(tonemap, vec3(l), desaturateAmount);

003d9070	a	            break;
003d9083	a	        }

003d90a0	a	        case TONEMAP_MODE_BREAK_2:
003d90c3	a	        case TONEMAP_MODE_BREAK:
003d90e4	a	        case TONEMAP_MODE_NONE:
003d9104	a	        default:
003d9115	a	            break;

003d9188	a	float rand(vec2 co) { return fract(sin(dot(co.xy ,vec2(12.9898,78.233))) * 43758.5453) * 0.0035; }
003d91ec	a	vec3 overlayV(vec3 bg, vec3 fg) {
003d920e	a	    return mix(2.0 * bg * fg, 1.0 - 2.0 * (1.0 - bg) * (1.0 - fg), step(0.5, bg));
003d9264	a	vec3 softLightV(vec3 a, vec3 b) { return ((vec3(1) - (vec3(2) * b)) * pow(a, vec3(2))) + (vec3(2) * b * a); } // pegtop formula

003d9300	a	vec3 aces_filmic(vec3 x) {
003d931b	a	    float a = 2.51;
003d932f	a	    float b = 0.03;
003d9343	a	    float c = 2.43;
003d9357	a	    float d = 0.59;
003d936b	a	    float e = 0.14;
003d937f	a	    return clamp((x * (a * x + b)) / (x * (c * x + d) + e), 0.0, 1.0);
003d93c9	a	vec3 filmic(vec3 x) {
003d93df	a	    vec3 X = max(vec3(0.0), x - 0.004);
003d9407	a	    vec3 result = (X * (6.2 * X + 0.5)) / (X * (6.2 * X + 1.7) + 0.06);
003d944f	a	    return pow(result, vec3(2.2)); // don't be fooled, this is correct
003d9499	a	vec3 reinhard2(vec3 x) {
003d94b2	a	    const float L_white = 4.0;
003d94d1	a	    return (x * (1.0 + x / (L_white * L_white))) / (1.0 + x);
003d9512	a	vec3 uncharted2_tonemap_partial(vec3 x)
003d953c	a	    float A = 0.15f;
003d9551	a	    float B = 0.50f;
003d9566	a	    float C = 0.10f;
003d957b	a	    float D = 0.20f;
003d9590	a	    float E = 0.02f;
003d95a5	a	    float F = 0.30f;
003d95bc	a	// Calculate intermediate values with higher precision
003d95f3	a	    //vec3 num = x*(A*x+C*B)+D*E;
003d9615	a	    //vec3 den = x*(A*x+B)+D*F;
003d9635	a	    //return (num/den)-E/F;
003d9653	a	// Add epsilon to prevent division by very small numbers
003d968c	a	    x = max(vec3(eps), x);
003d96a7	a	    return ((x*(A*x+C*B)+D*E)/(x*(A*x+B)+D*F))-E/F;
003d96de	a	vec3 uncharted2_filmic(vec3 v)
003d96ff	a	    float exposure_bias = 2.0f;
003d971f	a	    vec3 curr = uncharted2_tonemap_partial(v * exposure_bias);
003d975f	a	    vec3 W = vec3(11.2f);
003d9779	a	    vec3 white_scale = vec3(1.0f) / uncharted2_tonemap_partial(W);
003d97bc	a	    return curr * white_scale;
003d97e1	a	vec3 lottes(vec3 x) {
003d97f7	a	    const vec3 a = vec3(1.6);
003d9815	a	    const vec3 d = vec3(0.977);
003d9835	a	    const vec3 hdrMax = vec3(8.0);
003d9858	a	    const vec3 midIn = vec3(0.18);
003d987b	a	    const vec3 midOut = vec3(0.267);
003d98a1	a	    const vec3 b =
003d98b4	a	    (-pow(midIn, a) + pow(hdrMax, a) * midOut) /
003d98e5	a	    ((pow(hdrMax, a * d) - pow(midIn, a * d)) * midOut);
003d991e	a	    const vec3 c =
003d9931	a	    (pow(hdrMax, a * d) * pow(midIn, a) - pow(hdrMax, a) * pow(midIn, a * d) * midOut) /
003d998a	a	    ((pow(hdrMax, a * d) - pow(midIn, a * d)) * midOut);
003d99c4	a	    return pow(x, a) / (pow(x, a * d) * b + c);
003d99f7	a	vec3 uchimura(vec3 x, float P, float a, float m, float l, float c, float b) {
003d9a45	a	    float l0 = ((P - m) * l) / a;
003d9a67	a	    float L0 = m - m / a;
003d9a81	a	    float L1 = m + (1.0 - m) / a;
003d9aa3	a	    float S0 = m + l0;
003d9aba	a	    float S1 = m + a * l0;
003d9ad5	a	    float C2 = (a * P) / (P - S1);
003d9af8	a	    float CP = -C2 / P;
003d9b11	a	    vec3 w0 = vec3(1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, m, x));
003d9b42	a	    vec3 w2 = vec3(step(m + l0, x));
003d9b67	a	    vec3 w1 = vec3(1.0 - w0 - w2);
003d9b8b	a	    vec3 T = vec3(m * pow(x / m, vec3(c)) + b);
003d9bbb	a	    vec3 S = vec3(P - (P - S1) * exp(CP * (x - S0)));
003d9bf1	a	    vec3 L = vec3(m + a * (x - m));
003d9c16	a	    return T * w0 + L * w1 + S * w2;
003d9c3e	a	vec3 uchimura(vec3 x) {
003d9c56	a	    const float P = 1.0;  // max display brightness
003d9c8a	a	    const float a = 1.0;  // contrast
003d9cb0	a	    const float m = 0.22; // linear section start
003d9ce2	a	    const float l = 0.4;  // linear section length
003d9d15	a	    const float c = 1.33; // black
003d9d38	a	    const float b = 0.0;  // pedestal
003d9d5f	a	    return uchimura(x, P, a, m, l, c, b);

003da148	a	float rgb2s(vec3 c)
003da15e	a	    float maxVal = max(max(c.r, c.g), c.b);
003da18a	a	    if (maxVal > 1.0)
003da1a0	a	    {
003da1a6	a	        c /= maxVal;
003da1bb	a	    }
003da1c2	a	    float minVal = min(min(c.r, c.g), c.b);
003da1ee	a	    float delta = maxVal - minVal;
003da211	a	    float s = 0.0;
003da225	a	    if (maxVal != 0.0)
003da23c	a	    {
003da242	a	        s = delta / maxVal;
003da25e	a	    }
003da265	a	    return s;

003db39f	a	// we need in and out res for this, higher quality at high downsampling
003db3e7	a	uniform vec2 in_res;
003db3fc	a	uniform vec2 out_res;
003db413	a	in vec2 out_uvs;
003db424	a	out vec3 colourOut;
003db439	a	void main()
003db447	a	    // Calculate aspect ratio correction
003db470	a	    vec2 aspectRatio = in_res / out_res;
003db49a	a	    // Calculate texel size
003db4b6	a	    vec2 texelSize = 1.0 / in_res;
003db4da	a	    // Calculate adjusted offsets
003db4fc	a	    vec2 offset = texelSize * aspectRatio;
003db528	a	    // Take 9 samples around current texel:
003db554	a	    // a - b - c
003db565	a	    // d - e - f
003db576	a	    // g - h - i
003db587	a	    // === ('e' is the current texel) ===
003db5b1	a	    vec3 a = texture(videoTexture, out_uvs + vec2(-offset.x,  offset.y)).rgb;
003db5ff	a	    vec3 b = texture(videoTexture, out_uvs + vec2(0,          offset.y)).rgb;
003db64d	a	    vec3 c = texture(videoTexture, out_uvs + vec2( offset.x,  offset.y)).rgb;
003db69c	a	    vec3 d = texture(videoTexture, out_uvs + vec2(-offset.x,  0)).rgb;
003db6e3	a	    vec3 e = texture(videoTexture, out_uvs).rgb;
003db714	a	    vec3 f = texture(videoTexture, out_uvs + vec2( offset.x,  0)).rgb;
003db75c	a	    vec3 g = texture(videoTexture, out_uvs + vec2(-offset.x, -offset.y)).rgb;
003db7aa	a	    vec3 h = texture(videoTexture, out_uvs + vec2(0,         -offset.y)).rgb;
003db7f8	a	    vec3 i = texture(videoTexture, out_uvs + vec2( offset.x, -offset.y)).rgb;
003db847	a	    // Apply weighted distribution, using a 3x3 tent filter:
003db884	a	    //  1   | 1 2 1 |
003db89a	a	    // -- * | 2 4 2 |
003db8b0	a	    // 16   | 1 2 1 |
003db8c6	a	    vec3 upsample = e * 4.0;
003db8e3	a	    upsample += (b + d + f + h) * 2.0;
003db90a	a	    upsample += (a + c + g + i);
003db92b	a	    upsample *= 1.0 / 16.0;
003db94c	a	    colourOut = upsample;
003db968	a	// end shader

003dba24	a	    out_uvs = tex;
